          -55.627 c 30.5891,-30.59485 56.1806,-55.627 56.8701,-55.627 0.6894,
          0 79.8637,78.60862 175.9427,174.68583 l 174.6892,174.6858 174.6892,
          -174.6858 c 96.079,-96.07721 175.253196,-174.68583 175.942696,
          -174.68583 0.6895,0 26.281,25.03215 56.8701,
          55.627 l 55.6165,55.627 -231.245496,231.24803 c -127.185,127.1864
          -231.5279,231.248 -231.873,231.248 -0.3451,0 -104.688,
          -104.0616 -231.873,-231.248 z
        " fill="currentColor"></path></g></svg><svg class="" viewBox="0 0 926.23699 573.74994" version="1.1" x="0px" y="0px" width="15" height="15" style="transform: translate(2px, 4px); transition: transform 0.2s;"><g transform="translate(904.92214,-879.1482)"><path d="
          m -673.67664,1221.6502 -231.2455,-231.24803 55.6165,
          -55.627 c 30.5891,-30.59485 56.1806,-55.627 56.8701,-55.627 0.6894,
          0 79.8637,78.60862 175.9427,174.68583 l 174.6892,174.6858 174.6892,
          -174.6858 c 96.079,-96.07721 175.253196,-174.68583 175.942696,
          -174.68583 0.6895,0 26.281,25.03215 56.8701,
          55.627 l 55.6165,55.627 -231.245496,231.24803 c -127.185,127.1864
          -231.5279,231.248 -231.873,231.248 -0.3451,0 -104.688,
          -104.0616 -231.873,-231.248 z
        " fill="currentColor"></path></g></svg></div></div><div class="sc-eulNck dQiXRu api-content"><div class="sc-dcJsrY eVrqat"><div class="sc-kAyceB XMnSL"><div class="sc-fqkvVR cEAxIC api-info"><h1 class="sc-imWYAI sc-ktJbId dCEJze kxOCzH">Arvancloud IaaS API Documentation <span>(v3.0.1)</span></h1><p>Download OpenAPI specification:<a download="" target="_blank" href="https://www.arvancloud.ir/api-docs/iaas-3.0.0.yaml" rel="noreferrer" class="sc-gmgFlS gDfmoL">Download</a></p><div class="sc-eeDRCY sc-eBMEME gvJSKt hzUya"><div class="sc-fiCwlc ePZoYv"><div class="sc-jIGnZt la-DwZT"><span class="sc-kMkxaj gCZfXY">API Support: <a href="mailto:callcenter@arvancloud.ir">callcenter@arvancloud.ir</a></span>   </div></div></div><div class="sc-eeDRCY sc-eBMEME gvJSKt hzUya" data-role="redoc-summary"></div><div class="sc-eeDRCY sc-eBMEME gvJSKt hzUya" data-role="redoc-description"><p>Welcome to the official documentation for the Arvancloud Infrastructure as a Service (IaaS) API.</p>
<p>This API allows you to interact programmatically with Arvancloud’s IaaS platform. You can manage compute resources such as virtual machines (VMs), storage, networking, and security groups, as well as perform various administrative tasks.</p>
<p>The documentation provides all the details you need to authenticate, interact with, and utilize the API effectively. Whether you're building automation tools, integrating Arvancloud into your existing workflows, or managing resources through a third-party platform, this API offers everything you need to get started.</p>
<p>This documentation covers the <strong>latest version</strong> of the Arvancloud IaaS API, with all the latest features, improvements, and bug fixes.</p>
<p>For any questions or assistance, please refer to our support team or community forums.</p>
</div></div></div></div><div id="tag/Introduction" data-section-id="tag/Introduction" class="sc-dcJsrY eVrqat"><div class="sc-kAyceB XMnSL"><div class="sc-fqkvVR cEAxIC"><h2 class="sc-jXbUNg copjkU"><a class="sc-jlZhew jrsfjb" href="#tag/Introduction" aria-label="tag/Introduction"></a>Introduction</h2></div></div><div class="sc-fqkvVR wnFnm"><div class="sc-eeDRCY sc-eBMEME gvJSKt hzUya redoc-markdown "><p>Welcome to the API documentation! This section provides an overview of how to use the API, including details on filtering, sorting, and pagination.</p>
</div></div></div><div id="tag/Introduction/Filtering" data-section-id="tag/Introduction/Filtering" class="sc-dcJsrY eVrqat"><div class="sc-kAyceB XMnSL"><div class="sc-fqkvVR cEAxIC"><h2 class="sc-dhKdcB cjhZre"><a class="sc-jlZhew jrsfjb" href="#tag/Introduction/Filtering" aria-label="tag/Introduction/Filtering"></a>Filtering</h2></div></div><div class="sc-fqkvVR wnFnm"><div class="sc-eeDRCY sc-eBMEME gvJSKt hzUya redoc-markdown "><p>The API allows you to filter results based on specific fields and conditions. Filtering helps you narrow down results to match your criteria.</p>
<h3 id="how-to-filter-data">How to Filter Data</h3>
<p>Filters are applied in the URL query string using the following format:</p>
<pre><code><span class="token operator">/</span>path<span class="token operator">/</span>to<span class="token operator">/</span>resources<span class="token operator">?</span><span class="token punctuation">{</span>field<span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token punctuation">{</span>operator<span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span><span class="token punctuation">{</span>value<span class="token punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre>
<ul>
<li><code>{field}</code>: The field you want to filter by (e.g., <code>status</code>, <code>createDate</code>).</li>
<li><code>{operator}</code>: The comparison operator (e.g., <code>eq</code>, <code>gt</code>, <code>lte</code>).</li>
<li><code>{value}</code>: The value to compare against.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="supported-operators">Supported Operators</h3>
<p>Here are the available operators for filtering:</p>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>eq</code></strong>: Equals to (e.g., <code>field=eq:value</code>)</li>
<li><strong><code>neq</code></strong>: Not equals to (e.g., <code>field=neq:value</code>)</li>
<li><strong><code>gt</code></strong>: Greater than (e.g., <code>field=gt:value</code>)</li>
<li><strong><code>gte</code></strong>: Greater than or equal to (e.g., <code>field=gte:value</code>)</li>
<li><strong><code>lt</code></strong>: Less than (e.g., <code>field=lt:value</code>)</li>
<li><strong><code>lte</code></strong>: Less than or equal to (e.g., <code>field=lte:value</code>)</li>
<li><strong><code>in</code></strong>: Matches any value in a list (e.g., <code>field=in:value1,value2,value3</code>)</li>
<li><strong><code>nin</code></strong>: Does not match any value in a list (e.g., <code>field=nin:value1,value2,value3</code>)</li>
</ul>
<blockquote>
<p><strong>Note:</strong> When using <code>in</code> or <code>nin</code>, provide a <strong>comma-separated list</strong> of values.</p>
</blockquote>
<h3 id="example-requests">Example Requests</h3>
<ul>
<li><p>To find items where <code>field1</code> equals "VALUE" and <code>field2</code> is greater than 10:</p>
<pre><code>GET <span class="token operator">/</span>path<span class="token operator">/</span>to<span class="token operator">/</span>resources<span class="token operator">?</span>field1<span class="token operator">=</span>eq<span class="token punctuation">:</span>VALUE<span class="token operator">&amp;</span>field2<span class="token operator">=</span>gt<span class="token punctuation">:</span><span class="token number">10</span>
</code></pre>
</li>
<li><p>To get servers with specific <code>id</code> values:</p>
<pre><code>GET <span class="token operator">/</span>v3<span class="token operator">/</span>servers<span class="token operator">?</span>id<span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token keyword">in</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span>a7f147d7<span class="token operator">-</span><span class="token number">474</span>d<span class="token operator">-</span><span class="token number">53</span>de<span class="token operator">-</span><span class="token number">8</span>f98<span class="token operator">-</span><span class="token number">25</span>ceaaaac5d1<span class="token punctuation">,</span>eda8c2c0<span class="token operator">-</span><span class="token number">9</span>bc1<span class="token operator">-</span><span class="token number">5</span>d0d<span class="token operator">-</span><span class="token number">953</span>f<span class="token operator">-</span><span class="token number">80122114261</span>f
</code></pre>
</li>
</ul>
</div></div></div><div id="tag/Introduction/Sorting" data-section-id="tag/Introduction/Sorting" class="sc-dcJsrY eVrqat"><div class="sc-kAyceB XMnSL"><div class="sc-fqkvVR cEAxIC"><h2 class="sc-dhKdcB cjhZre"><a class="sc-jlZhew jrsfjb" href="#tag/Introduction/Sorting" aria-label="tag/Introduction/Sorting"></a>Sorting</h2></div></div><div class="sc-fqkvVR wnFnm"><div class="sc-eeDRCY sc-eBMEME gvJSKt hzUya redoc-markdown "><p>Sorting allows you to order the results based on one or more fields, in either ascending or descending order.</p>
<h3 id="how-to-sort-data">How to Sort Data</h3>
<p>To apply sorting, use the <code>sortBy</code> parameter in the query string. Specify fields to sort by, followed by the order (<code>asc</code> or <code>desc</code>).</p>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>asc</code></strong>: Ascending order (lowest to highest).</li>
<li><strong><code>desc</code></strong>: Descending order (highest to lowest).</li>
</ul>
<p>You can sort by multiple fields. For example, sorting by <code>userId</code> in ascending order and <code>registerDate</code> in descending order:</p>
<h3 id="example-request">Example Request:</h3>
<pre><code>GET <span class="token operator">/</span>v1<span class="token operator">/</span>users<span class="token operator">?</span>sortBy<span class="token operator">=</span>asc<span class="token punctuation">:</span>userId<span class="token punctuation">,</span>desc<span class="token punctuation">:</span>registerDate
</code></pre>
</div></div></div><div id="tag/Introduction/Status-Codes" data-section-id="tag/Introduction/Status-Codes" class="sc-dcJsrY eVrqat"><div class="sc-kAyceB XMnSL"><div class="sc-fqkvVR cEAxIC"><h2 class="sc-dhKdcB cjhZre"><a class="sc-jlZhew jrsfjb" href="#tag/Introduction/Status-Codes" aria-label="tag/Introduction/Status-Codes"></a>Status Codes</h2></div></div><div class="sc-fqkvVR wnFnm"><div class="sc-eeDRCY sc-eBMEME gvJSKt hzUya redoc-markdown "><p>The HTTP status code in the response indicates the result of your request. Here’s a list of common status codes and their meanings:</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Status Code</th>
<th>Meaning</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody><tr>
<td><strong>200</strong></td>
<td>The request was successful and the resource was returned (usually for <code>GET</code> requests).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>201</strong></td>
<td>A resource was successfully created (usually after a <code>POST</code> request).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>202</strong></td>
<td>The request to change a resource was accepted (for <code>PUT</code> or <code>PATCH</code> requests).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>204</strong></td>
<td>The request was successful, but there is no content in the response (e.g., after a <code>DELETE</code> request).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>400</strong></td>
<td>There was an error in your request, but it's not a specific error defined in the status codes.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>401</strong></td>
<td>Authentication failed. You need to provide valid credentials.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>402</strong></td>
<td>Payment is required (rarely used).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>403</strong></td>
<td>You don't have permission to access this resource.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>404</strong></td>
<td>The resource you requested was not found.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>405</strong></td>
<td>The method you're using (e.g., <code>GET</code>, <code>POST</code>) is not allowed for the given URL.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>409</strong></td>
<td>There was a conflict while trying to modify the resource.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>410</strong></td>
<td>The resource is no longer available or has been permanently removed.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>415</strong></td>
<td>There’s a conflict with an existing resource that prevents the action.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>422</strong></td>
<td>There was a validation error with your input.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>429</strong></td>
<td>You have exceeded the rate limit and need to wait before making more requests.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>500</strong></td>
<td>There was an internal error on the server.</td>
</tr>
</tbody></table>
</div></div></div><div id="tag/Introduction/Response" data-section-id="tag/Introduction/Response" class="sc-dcJsrY eVrqat"><div class="sc-kAyceB XMnSL"><div class="sc-fqkvVR cEAxIC"><h2 class="sc-dhKdcB cjhZre"><a class="sc-jlZhew jrsfjb" href="#tag/Introduction/Response" aria-label="tag/Introduction/Response"></a>Response</h2></div></div><div class="sc-fqkvVR wnFnm"><div class="sc-eeDRCY sc-eBMEME gvJSKt hzUya redoc-markdown "><h3 id="headers">Headers</h3>
<p>The headers in the response contain additional information about the request and response. Some of the headers are <strong>required</strong>, while others are <strong>optional</strong>. Here are a few important ones:</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Header Name</th>
<th>Description</th>
<th>Example</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody><tr>
<td><strong><code>Sunset</code></strong></td>
<td>Indicates if the API or resource is deprecated, with a date of removal.</td>
<td><code>2024-01-01T00:00:00Z</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong><code>X-AR-Request-Id</code></strong></td>
<td>A unique identifier for your request, used for tracking and ensuring idempotency.</td>
<td><code>4da5d7c1-9f87-5975-8f2d-d6f3c939e513</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody></table>
<h3 id="body-format">Body Format</h3>
<p>The response body is typically in JSON format and contains the data you're looking for, along with any additional information like errors or metadata.</p>
<p>Here’s the general structure of a JSON response:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="token punctuation">{</span>
  <span class="token string-property property">"message"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"A human-readable message about the response"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="token string-property property">"data"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"The actual data (an array, object, etc.) representing the resource(s)"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="token string-property property">"errors"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span>
    <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
      <span class="token string-property property">"reason"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"The error reason"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
      <span class="token string-property property">"field"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"The field that caused the error (optional)"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
      <span class="token string-property property">"params"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"Additional details about the error (optional)"</span>
    <span class="token punctuation">}</span>
  <span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="token string-property property">"meta"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"Additional metadata about the response (optional)"</span>
<span class="token punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre>
<ul>
<li>The <code>message</code> provides a summary of the response.</li>
<li>The <code>data</code> contains the requested resource(s).</li>
<li>If there was an error with your request, the <code>errors</code> field will provide details.</li>
<li>The <code>meta</code> field contains any extra information about the response, such as pagination details.</li>
</ul>
<h4 id="error-format">Error Format</h4>
<p>When there’s an error, it will be returned in the following format:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="token string-property property">"errors"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span>
  <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
    <span class="token string-property property">"reason"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"The error reason"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string-property property">"field"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"The field that caused the error (if applicable)"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
    <span class="token string-property property">"params"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"Additional details about the error (if applicable)"</span>
  <span class="token punctuation">}</span>
<span class="token punctuation">]</span>
</code></pre>
<ul>
<li><strong>Reason</strong>: A description of the error (e.g., <code>INVALID_INPUT</code>, <code>MISSING_FIELD</code>).</li>
<li><strong>Field</strong>: The specific field causing the error, if applicable (e.g., <code>username</code>).</li>
<li><strong>Params</strong>: Extra details about the error, such as validation constraints.</li>
</ul>
<blockquote>
<p><strong>Note:</strong> Error reasons will be listed in the API documentation, so you can easily look up and understand the meaning of any error.</p>
</blockquote>
</div></div></div><div id="tag/Introduction/Pagination" data-section-id="tag/Introduction/Pagination" class="sc-dcJsrY eVrqat"><div class="sc-kAyceB XMnSL"><div class="sc-fqkvVR cEAxIC"><h2 class="sc-dhKdcB cjhZre"><a class="sc-jlZhew jrsfjb" href="#tag/Introduction/Pagination" aria-label="tag/Introduction/Pagination"></a>Pagination</h2></div></div><div class="sc-fqkvVR wnFnm"><div class="sc-eeDRCY sc-eBMEME gvJSKt hzUya redoc-markdown "><p>When requesting a list of resources, the response may include pagination information to help you retrieve the data in smaller chunks.</p>
<p>There are two types of pagination available: <strong>offset-based</strong> and <strong>cursor-based</strong>.</p>
<h3 id="offset-based-pagination">Offset-based Pagination</h3>
<p>This is a common pagination method where you specify the page number and the number of items per page.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>perPage</code></strong>: Specifies the number of items per page.</li>
<li><strong><code>page</code></strong>: Specifies which page to retrieve.</li>
</ul>
<p>Here’s an example response with offset-based pagination:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="token punctuation">{</span>
  <span class="token string-property property">"message"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"Resources found"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="token string-property property">"data"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token operator">...</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="token string-property property">"meta"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
    <span class="token string-property property">"pagination"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
      <span class="token string-property property">"perPage"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">10</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
      <span class="token string-property property">"current"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">1</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
      <span class="token string-property property">"totalItems"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">100</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
      <span class="token string-property property">"lastPage"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">10</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
      <span class="token string-property property">"next"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">2</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
      <span class="token string-property property">"previous"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token keyword">null</span>
    <span class="token punctuation">}</span>
  <span class="token punctuation">}</span>
<span class="token punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>perPage</code></strong>: Number of items per page (e.g., 10).</li>
<li><strong><code>current</code></strong>: The current page number.</li>
<li><strong><code>totalItems</code></strong>: The total number of items available.</li>
<li><strong><code>lastPage</code></strong>: The last page number.</li>
<li><strong><code>next</code></strong>: The next page number (if applicable).</li>
<li><strong><code>previous</code></strong>: The previous page number (if applicable).</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="cursor-based-pagination">Cursor-based Pagination</h3>
<p>This pagination method is preferred when dealing with large datasets. It uses a unique token to track pagination, rather than using page numbers.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>perPage</code></strong>: Specifies the number of items per page.</li>
<li><strong><code>nextPageToken</code></strong>: A unique token to fetch the next page of results.</li>
</ul>
<p>Here’s an example response with cursor-based pagination:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="token punctuation">{</span>
  <span class="token string-property property">"message"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"Resources found"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="token string-property property">"data"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token operator">...</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="token string-property property">"meta"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
    <span class="token string-property property">"pagination"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span>
      <span class="token string-property property">"perPage"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token number">10</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span>
      <span class="token string-property property">"nextPageToken"</span><span class="token operator">:</span> <span class="token string">"&lt;Random Token&gt;|null"</span>
    <span class="token punctuation">}</span>
  <span class="token punctuation">}</span>
<span class="token punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>nextPageToken</code></strong>: The unique token used to retrieve the next set of results. If there are no more results, this token will be <code>null</code>.</li>
</ul>
<blockquote>
<p><strong>Example Request</strong>: <code>/v1/users?perPage=10&amp;nextPageToken=TLJgsnhcmUh</code></p>
</blockquote>
<p>The <code>nextPageToken</code> refers to a unique, sequential key in your database that helps keep track of the pages.</p>
</div></div></div><div id="tag/Authentication" data-section-id="tag/Authentication" class="sc-dcJsrY eVrqat"><div class="sc-kAyceB XMnSL"><div class="sc-fqkvVR cEAxIC"><h2 class="sc-jXbUNg copjkU"><a class="sc-jlZhew jrsfjb" href="#tag/Authentication" aria-label="tag/Authentication"></a>Authentication</h2></div></div><div class="sc-fqkvVR wnFnm"><div class="sc-eeDRCY sc-eBMEME gvJSKt hzUya redoc-markdown "><p>This section explains how to authenticate API requests. The API supports two methods of authentication: <strong>API Key</strong> and <strong>Bearer Token</strong>. These methods must be included in the <code>Authorization</code> header of your requests.</p>
</div></div></div><div id="tag/Authentication/API-Key" data-section-id="tag/Authentication/API-Key" class="sc-dcJsrY eVrqat"><div class="sc-kAyceB XMnSL"><div class="sc-fqkvVR cEAxIC"><h2 class="sc-dhKdcB cjhZre"><a class="sc-jlZhew jrsfjb" href="#tag/Authentication/API-Key" aria-label="tag/Authentication/API-Key"></a>API Key</h2></div></div><div class="sc-fqkvVR wnFnm"><div class="sc-eeDRCY sc-eBMEME gvJSKt hzUya redoc-markdown "><p>To authenticate using an API key, include the API key in the <code>Authorization</code> header in the following format:</p>
<pre><code>Authorization<span class="token punctuation">:</span> apikey <span class="token punctuation">{</span>uuid<span class="token punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>{uuid}</code></strong>: Your unique API key. You can obtain this key from the API provider's dashboard or management interface.</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Example Request</strong>:</p>
<pre><code>GET <span class="token operator">/</span>v1<span class="token operator">/</span>resources
Authorization<span class="token punctuation">:</span> apikey <span class="token number">123e4567</span><span class="token operator">-</span>e89b<span class="token operator">-</span><span class="token number">12</span>d3<span class="token operator">-</span>a456<span class="token operator">-</span><span class="token number">426614174000</span>
</code></pre>
</div></div></div><div id="tag/Authentication/Bearer-Token" data-section-id="tag/Authentication/Bearer-Token" class="sc-dcJsrY eVrqat"><div class="sc-kAyceB XMnSL"><div class="sc-fqkvVR cEAxIC"><h2 class="sc-dhKdcB cjhZre"><a class="sc-jlZhew jrsfjb" href="#tag/Authentication/Bearer-Token" aria-label="tag/Authentication/Bearer-Token"></a>Bearer Token</h2></div></div><div class="sc-fqkvVR wnFnm"><div class="sc-eeDRCY sc-eBMEME gvJSKt hzUya redoc-markdown "><p>Alternatively, you can authenticate using a Bearer token. To do so, include the token in the <code>Authorization</code> header in the following format:</p>
<pre><code>Authorization<span class="token punctuation">:</span> Bearer <span class="token punctuation">{</span>token<span class="token punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>{token}</code></strong>: The Bearer token you receive after successfully authenticating via the login or token endpoint. This token is typically short-lived, and you may need to refresh it periodically.</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Example Request</strong>:</p>
<pre><code>GET <span class="token operator">/</span>v1<span class="token operator">/</span>resources
Authorization<span class="token punctuation">:</span> Bearer abcdef1234567890
</code></pre>
<p>Make sure that your API key or Bearer token is kept secure, as it grants access to sensitive data and actions on the API.</p>
</div></div></div><div id="tag/AvailabilityZones" data-section-id="tag/AvailabilityZones" class="sc-dcJsrY eVrqat"><div class="sc-kAyceB XMnSL"><div class="sc-fqkvVR cEAxIC"><h2 class="sc-jXbUNg copjkU"><a class="sc-jlZhew jrsfjb" href="#tag/AvailabilityZones" aria-label="tag/AvailabilityZones"></a>AvailabilityZones</h2></div></div><div class="sc-fqkvVR wnFnm"><div class="sc-eeDRCY sc-eBMEME gvJSKt hzUya redoc-markdown "><p>Endpoints for listing Availability Zones data and status. </p>
</div></div></div><div id="tag/Servers" data-section-id="tag/Servers" class="sc-dcJsrY eVrqat"><div class="sc-kAyceB XMnSL"><div class="sc-fqkvVR cEAxIC"><h2 class="sc-jXbUNg copjkU"><a class="sc-jlZhew jrsfjb" href="#tag/Servers" aria-label="tag/Servers"></a>Servers</h2></div></div><div class="sc-fqkvVR wnFnm"><div class="sc-eeDRCY sc-eBMEME gvJSKt hzUya redoc-markdown "><p>Endpoints for managing compute instances (servers) in the application. Allows you to create, update, delete, and retrieve details about virtual machines (VMs), including their status, configuration, and metadata. Features include server creation from backups, managing server volumes, resizing, renaming, and performing rescue operations. You can also manage SSH keys, security groups, and network interfaces for servers.</p>
</div></div></div><div id="tag/Servers/operation/ListServers" data-section-id="tag/Servers/operation/ListServers" class="sc-dcJsrY kNjBFu"><div data-section-id="operation/ListServers" id="operation/ListServers" class="sc-kAyceB XMnSL"><div class="sc-fqkvVR cEAxIC"><h2 class="sc-jXbUNg copjkU"><a class="sc-jlZhew jrsfjb" href="#tag/Servers/operation/ListServers" aria-label="tag/Servers/operation/ListServers"></a>List Servers </h2><div class="sc-bVVIoq bxAVpD"><div class="sc-eeDRCY sc-eBMEME gvJSKt hzUya"><p>List all servers of the tenant within the region</p>
</div></div><div class="sc-jaXxmE cDocAe"><div class="sc-iMTnTL fJRIzN"><h5 class="sc-dAlyuH sc-hwdzOV hNlDMA hjazRA">Authorizations:</h5><svg class="sc-cwHptR kNXOKB" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 24 24" x="0" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" y="0" aria-hidden="true"><polygon points="17.3 8.3 12 13.6 6.7 8.3 5.3 9.7 12 16.4 18.7 9.7 "></polygon></svg></div><div class="sc-krNlru jiAhSp"><span class="sc-jMakVo ezhZdr">None</span><span class="sc-jMakVo ezhZdr">None</span></div></div><div><h5 class="sc-dAlyuH hNlDMA">query Parameters</h5><table class="sc-dAbbOL krDyRf"><tbody><tr class=""><td class="sc-dLMFU sc-eldPxv bvNJXm evxOdD" kind="field" title="page"><span class="sc-gFqAkR Aouot"></span><span class="property-name">page</span></td><td class="sc-fPXMVe iMGXTw"><div><div><span class="sc-Nxspf sc-cfxfcM lmPAIU bZSMCf"></span><span class="sc-Nxspf sc-gFAWRd lmPAIU iHgalF">integer</span></div> <div><div class="sc-eeDRCY sc-eBMEME gvJSKt FDQvA"><p>Page number</p>

</div></div></div></td></tr><tr class=""><td class="sc-dLMFU sc-eldPxv bvNJXm evxOdD" kind="field" title="enableIpv4"><span class="sc-gFqAkR Aouot"></span><span class="property-name">enableIpv4</span></td><td class="sc-fPXMVe iMGXTw"><div><div><span class="sc-Nxspf sc-cfxfcM lmPAIU bZSMCf"></span><span class="sc-Nxspf sc-gFAWRd lmPAIU iHgalF">boolean</span></div> <div><div class="sc-eeDRCY sc-eBMEME gvJSKt FDQvA"><p>enable IPv4 on the server, this is required to assign IPv6 addresses to the server</p>

</div></div></div></div><div class="sc-dExYaf gwMRTQ"></div></div>
